# Remaining lookup-table definitions - issue 1165

Remaining: **1128 canonical table definitions in 523 source files**.

Count one named source table definition, including a factory-built or installed stock table, once. Do not count its rows, individual loaded instances, serialization buffers, aggregate views, aliases or independent value columns again. For example, Torizo Frames and Hitboxes are two definitions, not 126 nested arrays. A generic stock-payload entry covers its complete resource-identity domain. Compound entries explicitly require separate dispositions for their independently indexed value fields; this total is a table-definition count, not a count of those finer conversion steps or physical ROM blocks.

Every listed definition still needs conversion or a concrete impossible/nonsense justification for its unresolved stock values. A pending entry is not a claim that a mathematical generator exists. Performance, complexity, size and unknown historical-tool provenance are not exceptions. Arbitrary installed overrides remain editable; they are not additional original-game tables.

Scope: shipped gameplay, menus, cinematics, rendering/audio behavior and stock runtime data. Tools, tests, verification fixtures, diagnostics, port-only schema/configuration and reference sources are excluded as targets. Factories, packed data, auto-properties, embedded level data and installed content were included; existing algorithms and semantic cases are not counted merely because they contain switches.
